The default 15s is fine\n// for the small create/poll/transcript calls, but an upload that re-sends the\n// whole recording (AssemblyAI /v2/upload, Soniox /v1/files, Azure MAI multipart)\n// can far exceed 15s for the large files these async/large-cap providers accept —\n// aborting mid-upload would surface as a spurious 502. Floor 30s + 1s per 100 KB.\n// Mirrors gcs-storage's GCS upload budget.\nconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS = 30_000;\nconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS = 1_000;\nexport function computeUploadTimeoutMs(byteLength: number): number {\n  return Math.max(UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS, Math.ceil(byteLength / 100_000) * UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS);\n}\n\n/**\n * Conservative audio-duration estimate (seconds) from the encoded byte length,\n * for fail-closed billing when an upstream returns a successful transcript but\n * omits the duration/usage we'd normally bill on. Mirrors the route's preflight\n * size→seconds heuristic (64 kbps encoded).\n */\nexport function estimateSecondsFromBytes(byteLength: number): number {\n  return (byteLength / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE) * 60;\n}\n\nfunction resolveProviderTimeoutMs(): number {\n  const configured = Number.parseInt(process.env.STT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS || '', 10);\n  if (Number.isFinite(configured) && configured > 0) {\n    return configured;\n  }\n\n  return DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS;\n}\n\nfunction serializeError(error: unknown): string {\n  if (error instanceof Error) {\n    return error.message;\n  }\n\n  return String(error);\n}\n\nfunction isAbortError(error: unknown): boolean {\n  return error instanceof DOMException && error.name === 'AbortError';\n}\n\nexport function logProviderEvent(\n  provider: string,\n  event: string,\n  details: Record<string, unknown>,\n  context: ProviderRequestContext = {},\n) {\n  console.log(`provider.${event}`, {\n    provider,\n    requestId: context.requestId,\n    attempt: context.attempt,\n    ...details,\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function fetchWithTimeout(\n  provider: string,\n  url: string,\n  init: RequestInit,\n  context: ProviderRequestContext = {},\n  timeoutMsOverride?: number,\n): Promise<Response> {\n  const timeoutMs = typeof timeoutMsOverride === 'number' && timeoutMsOverride > 0\n    ? timeoutMsOverride\n    : resolveProviderTimeoutMs();\n  const startedAt = performance.now();\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  const timeoutHandle = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);\n\n  logProviderEvent(provider, 'request_start', { timeoutMs }, context);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(url, {\n      ...init,\n      signal: controller.signal,\n    });\n\n    logProviderEvent(provider, 'http_response', {\n      elapsedMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt),\n      status: response.status,\n      ok: response.ok,\n    }, context);\n\n    return response;\n  } catch (error) {\n    const elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);\n\n    if (isAbortError(error)) {\n      logProviderEvent(provider, 'transport_error', {\n        elapsedMs,\n        kind: 'timeout',\n        timeoutMs,\n      }, context);\n      throw new ProviderUnavailableError(provider, `timeout after ${timeoutMs}ms`, {\n        kind: 'timeout',\n        elapsedMs,\n      });\n    }\n\n    logProviderEvent(provider, 'transport_error', {\n      elapsedMs,\n      kind: 'network_error',\n      message: serializeError(error),\n    }, context);\n    throw new ProviderUnavailableError(provider, `network error: ${serializeError(error)}`, {\n      kind: 'network_error',\n      elapsedMs,\n    });\n  } finally {\n    clearTimeout(timeoutHandle);\n  }\n=====GCS\n/** True when `uploadTranscriptionAudio` can be used. */\nexport function isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured(): boolean {\n  return getConfiguredBucket() !== null;\n}\n\nfunction inferExtension(contentType: string): string {\n  const lower = contentType.toLowerCase();\n  if (lower.includes('wav')) return 'wav';\n  if (lower.includes('mp3') || lower.includes('mpeg')) return 'mp3';\n  if (lower.includes('m4a') || lower.includes('mp4')) return 'm4a';\n  if (lower.includes('webm')) return 'webm';\n  if (lower.includes('ogg') || lower.includes('opus')) return 'ogg';\n  if (lower.includes('flac')) return 'flac';\n  if (lower.includes('aac')) return 'aac';\n  return 'bin';\n}\n\nfunction buildObjectName(ext: string): string {\n  // Bun and Node both expose crypto.randomUUID() globally.\n  const uuid = crypto.randomUUID();\n  // The `stt-temp/` prefix lets the bucket's lifecycle rule Verified against OpenAI docs 2026-06-19.\n    16\t */\n    17\texport function buildOpenAIBody(payload: CorrectionRequestPayload, model: string): Record<string, unknown> {\n    18\t  const { temperature, ...rest } = payload;\n    19\t  return {\n    20\t    model,\n    21\t    ...rest,\n    22\t    reasoning_effort: 'minimal',\n    23\t    stream: false,\n    24\t  };\n    25\t}\n    26\t\n    27\texport async function requestOpenAIChat(\n    28\t  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n    29\t  requestId: string,\n    30\t  model: string\n    31\t): Promise<{ raw: unknown; usage?: GroqUsage; costUsd: number }> {\n    32\t  const apiKey = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY;\n    33\t  if (!apiKey) {\n    34\t    const error = new Error('OPENAI_API_KEY not configured');\n    35\t    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = 503;\n    36\t    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'openai';\n    37\t    throw error;\n    38\t  }\n    39\t\n    40\t  const response = await fetch(`${OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`, {\n    41\t    method: 'POST',\n    42\t    headers: {\n    43\t      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n    44\t      'content-type': 'application/json',\n    45\t    },\n    46\t    body: JSON.stringify(buildOpenAIBody(payload, model)),\n    47\t  });\n    48\t\n    49\t  if (!response.ok) {\n    50\t    const errorText = await safeReadText(response);\n    51\t    console.error('OpenAI API returned error', {\n    52\t      requestId,\n    53\t      status: response.status,\n    54\t      statusText: response.statusText,\n    55\t      errorText,\n    56\t    });\n    57\t    const error = new Error(`OpenAI chat failed with status ${response.status}`);\n    58\t    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = response.status;\n    59\t    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'openai';\n    60\t    throw error;\n    61\t  }\n    62\t\n    63\t  const json = await response.json();\n    64\t  const usage = isRecord(json) && isGroqUsage(json['usage']) ? (json['usage'] as GroqUsage) : undefined;\n    65\t  const costUsd = computeOpenAIChatCost(model, usage ?? reportMissingUsage('openai', payload, json, requestId));\n    66\t\n    67\t  return {\n    68\t    raw: json,\n    69\t    usage,\n    70\t    costUsd,\n    71\t  };\n    72\t}\n=========== gemini-llm\n     1\t// GEMINI LLM CLIENT (OPENAI-COMPATIBLE CHAT COMPLETIONS)\n     2\t\n     3\timport { computeGeminiChatCost, isGroqUsage, type GroqUsage } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\n     4\timport { isRecord, safeReadText } from '../lib/utils';\n     5\timport { reportMissingUsage, type CorrectionRequestPayload } from './groq-llm';\n     6\t\n     7\t// Gemini exposes an OpenAI-compatible surface that accepts the shared chat\n     8\t// payload unchanged. Verified 2026-06-19.\n     9\tconst GEMINI_BASE_URL = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai';\n    10\t\n    11\texport async function requestGeminiChat(\n    12\t  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n    13\t  requestId: string,\n    14\t  model: string\n    15\t): Promise<{ raw: unknown; usage?: GroqUsage; costUsd: number }> {\n    16\t  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || process.env.GOOGLE_GEMINI_API_KEY;\n    17\t  if (!apiKey) {\n    18\t    const error = new Error('GEMINI_API_KEY not configured');\n    19\t    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = 503;\n    20\t    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'gemini';\n    21\t    throw error;\n    22\t  }\n    23\t\n    24\t  // The 2.5-flash family defaults to a dynamic thinking budget and bills\n    25\t  // thinking tokens as output — wasted latency/cost for simple text cleanup.\n    26\t  // Gemini's OpenAI-compat surface disables thinking via reasoning_effort: 'none'.\n    27\t  const disableThinking = model.startsWith('gemini-2.5-flash');\n    28\t\n    29\t  const response = await fetch(`${GEMINI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`, {\n    30\t    method: 'POST',\n    31\t    headers: {\n    32\t      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n    33\t      'content-type': 'application/json',\n    34\t    },\n    35\t    body: JSON.stringify({\n    36\t      model,\n    37\t      ...payload,\n    38\t      ...(disableThinking ? { reasoning_effort: 'none' } : {}),\n    39\t      stream: false,\n    40\t    }),\n    41\t  });\n    42\t\n    43\t  if (!response.ok) {\n    44\t    const errorText = await safeReadText(response);\n    45\t    console.error('Gemini API returned error', {\n    46\t      requestId,\n    47\t      status: response.status,\n    48\t      statusText: response.statusText,\n    49\t      errorText,\n    50\t    });\n    51\t    const error = new Error(`Gemini chat failed with status ${response.status}`);\n    52\t    (error as { status?: number; provider?: string }).status = response.status;\n    53\t    (error as { provider?: string }).provider = 'gemini';\n    54\t    throw error;\n    55\t  }\n    56\t\n    57\t  const json = await response.json();\n    58\t  const usage = isRecord(json) && isGroqUsage(json['usage']) ? (json['usage'] as GroqUsage) : undefined;\n    59\t  const costUsd = computeGeminiChatCost(model, usage ?? reportMissingUsage('gemini', payload, json, requestId));\n    60\t\n    61\t  return {\n    62\t    raw: json,\n    63\t    usage,\n    64\t    costUsd,\n    65\t  };\n    66\t}\n=========== groq-llm\n     1\t// GROQ LLM CLIENT (CHAT COMPLETIONS)\n     2\t\n     3\timport { computeGroqChatCost, estimateUsageFromChars, isGroqUsage, type GroqUsage } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\n     4\timport { isRecord, safeReadText } from '../lib/utils';\n     5\t\n     6\tconst GROQ_BASE_URL = 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1';\n     7\tconst GROQ_CHAT_MODEL = 'openai/gpt-oss-120b';\n     8\t\n     9\texport type ChatMessage = {\n    10\t  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool';\n    11\t  content: string;\n    12\t};\n    13\t\n    14\texport type CorrectionRequestPayload = {\n    15\t  messages: ChatMessage[];\n    16\t  temperature: number;\n    17\t};\n    18\t\n    19\texport async function requestGroqChat(\n    20\t  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n    21\t  requestId: string\n    22\t): Promise<{ raw: unknown; usage?: GroqUsage; costUsd: number }> {\n    23\t  const apiKey = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;\n    24\t  if (!apiKey) {\n    25\t    throw new Error('GROQ_API_KEY not configured');\n    26\t  }\n    27\t\n    28\t  const chatUrl = `${GROQ_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`;\n    29\t\n    30\t  const response = await fetch(chatUrl, {\n    31\t    method: 'POST',\n    32\t    headers: {\n    33\t      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n    34\t      'content-type': 'application/json',\n    35\t    },\n    36\t    body: JSON.stringify({\n    37\t      model: GROQ_CHAT_MODEL,\n    38\t      ...payload,\n    39\t      reasoning_effort: 'low',\n    40\t      stream: false,\n    41\t    }),\n    42\t  });\n    43\t\n    44\t  if (!response.ok) {\n    45\t    const errorText = await safeReadText(response);\n    46\t    console.error('Groq LLM API returned error', {\n    47\t      requestId,\n    48\t      status: response.status,\n    49\t      statusText: response.statusText,\n    50\t      errorText,\n    51\t    });\n    52\t    const error = new Error(`Groq chat failed with status ${response.status}`);\n    53\t    (error as { status?: number; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; Used by the Google Chirp 3 STT provider AND\n// the GCS scratch bucket (lib/gcs-storage.ts) — both share one token.\n//\n// Cache key: `google_oauth_token`\n// TTL: derived from Google's returned `expiry_date` (capped at 1h), expiring\n//      10 minutes early so stragglers don't race the boundary. Each region/machine still has its own JWT client + in-flight\n// state — that's by design; cross-region coordination is what Redis is for.\n\nimport { JWT } from 'google-auth-library';\nimport { redis } from './redis';\n\nconst TOKEN_CACHE_KEY = 'google_oauth_token';\nconst FALLBACK_TTL_SECONDS = 3000;            // 50 min — used when Google omits expiry_date\nconst TOKEN_TTL_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS = 600;  // expire 10 min before Google's stated expiry\nconst MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 60;             // never cache shorter than 60s — pathological cases\nconst SPEECH_SCOPE = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform';\n\nlet _jwtClient: JWT | null = null;\nlet _inflight: Promise<string> | null = null;\n\nfunction getJwtClient(): JWT {\n  if (_jwtClient) {\n    return _jwtClient;\n  }\n\n  const raw = process.env.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON;\n  if (!raw) {\n    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON not configured');\n  }\n\n  let credentials: { client_email?: string; private_key?: string };\n  try {\n    credentials = JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch (error) {\n    throw new Error(`GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is not valid JSON: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);\n  }\n\n  if (!credentials.client_email || !credentials.private_key) {\n    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is missing client_email or private_key');\n  }\n\n  _jwtClient = new JWT({\n    email: credentials.client_email,\n    key: credentials.private_key,\n    scopes: [SPEECH_SCOPE],\n  });\n\n  return _jwtClient;\n}\n\nfunction computeCacheTtlSeconds(expiryDate: number | null | undefined): number {\n  if (typeof expiryDate !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(expiryDate)) {\n=== retriesFor sites\n(eval):1: no matches found: --include=*.ts","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"cb660ea9-f009-4921-8c8a-b12d57147f2c","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:36.459Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== google-auth head\n// GOOGLE OAUTH HELPER\n// Mints a short-lived Google Cloud access token from a service-account JSON\n// credential and caches it in Upstash Redis so the per-request cost is only\n// paid once per warm-up window. There is no `setTimeout`/`AbortSignal.timeout` wired to that controller, so a stalled-but-open upstream connection is never aborted.\nFailure scenario: A user sends a `POST /assistant` request with a screenshot. Anthropic accepts the connection, emits `message_start`, then stalls (TCP connection alive, no further SSE bytes — a known upstream degradation mode). The client is still attached, so `cancel()` never runs; `await reader.read()` (line 184) blocks indefinitely. The user's assistant panel spins forever with no error and no timeout. On the server, the 1 GB Fly machine holds the whole request scope — the buffered multipart body (`messages` up to 6 MB + a 10 MB image), the decoder buffer, the socket — plus `costPromise`, which never resolves, so the `costPromise.then(...)` deduction never fires and the already-billed Anthropic tokens are never charged. Repeated occurrences accumulate until OOM-kill, and every in-flight `/assistant` and `/transcribe` request on that machine dies with it.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 2\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/anthropic.ts`\nLine: 54\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `requestAnthropicChat` (non-streaming, used by `/post-process`) calls `fetch(ANTHROPIC_API_URL, {...})` with no `signal` at all.\nFailure scenario: A user dictates text with `X-LLM-Provider: anthropic`. Anthropic's `/v1/messages` accepts the POST and hangs. The `await fetch` never settles, so `retryWithBackoff` (`retriesFor('anthropic') === 2`) never even gets a chance to retry — there is no error to catch. The desktop client sits waiting on `/post-process` until its own client-side timeout, then shows a generic failure; the 1.0 credit reservation is consumed by a request that produced nothing. The Fly worker keeps the request scope (up to 100k chars of text plus the request/response objects) resident forever.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 3\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/groq-llm.ts`\nLine: 30\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `requestGroqChat` calls bare `fetch(chatUrl, {...})` with no `signal`.\nFailure scenario: Groq is the highest-retry provider (`retriesFor('groq') === 3`) and is a fallback target in `LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS`. When `api.groq.com` accepts connections but stalls (their documented behaviour under capacity pressure), a single `POST /post-process` pins one Bun request scope forever. Because the promise never rejects, the retry ladder, the provider fallback at `post-process.ts:149`, and the 500 error path at line 163 are all unreachable — the user never gets a response *or* an error, and the request never leaves memory. The request hangs indefinitely: no response, no error, no fallback to the configured fallback provider, and the reserved credit is burned for nothing. Server-side the scope leaks as above.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 6\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/gemini-llm.ts`\nLine: 29\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `requestGeminiChat` calls bare `fetch(`${GEMINI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`, {...})` with no `signal`.\nFailure scenario: Same shape — `generativelanguage.googleapis.com` stalls mid-request, `/post-process` never returns to the desktop client, and the request scope (including the full ≤100k-char text payload) is pinned on the Fly worker permanently. `retriesFor('gemini') === 2` is dead code in this scenario.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 7\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/mistral-llm.ts`\nLine: 24\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `requestMistralChat` calls bare `fetch(`${MISTRAL_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`, {...})` with no `signal`.\nFailure scenario: Identical stall-hang on `api.mistral.ai`; user-visible as a `/post-process` request that never completes and a consumed credit reservation with no output.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 8\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/xai-llm.ts`\nLine: 22\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `requestXaiGrokChat` calls bare `fetch(`${XAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions`, {...})` with no `signal`.\nFailure scenario: Identical stall-hang on `api.x.ai`; same permanently-pending `/post-process` response and leaked worker memory.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 9\nFile: `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`\nLine: 24\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `apiGet()` — the single helper behind all six provider list calls — does `fetch(url, { headers, cache: \"no-store\" })` with no `signal`. `fetchAnthropic` additionally calls it inside a `while (url)` pagination loop (line 69), so one stalled page hangs the loop.\nFailure scenario: This is the most exposed Next.js site: `GET /models` and `GET /api/internal/models` are both **unauthenticated, un-rate-limited** (I7 already flags the missing limiter) and both `export const dynamic = \"force-dynamic\"`. If any one of them accepts and stalls, `Promise.all` never settles and the Vercel function burns its full `maxDuration` before the platform kills it — the caller (the desktop client's model picker) gets a 504 rather than the graceful `{ ok: false, error }` per-provider degradation the code was written to produce, and the failure is then cached for `FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS` (60 s) so the model list is empty for every user for the next minute.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 10\nFile: `nextjs/server/api/routers/download.ts`\nLine: 90\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `getLatestDownloadUrl()` does `fetch(appcastUrl, { cache: \"no-store\" })` with no `signal` — note the sibling `app/api/download/route.ts:50` fetches the *same* appcast and *does* carry `AbortSignal.timeout(APPCAST_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)` (3 s). If the appcast origin stalls, the procedure hangs until the Vercel `maxDuration` ceiling, and the visitor's Download button sits in a loading state and then errors out instead of falling back to `null` after 3 s the way the REST route does. Unauthenticated and un-rate-limited, so it is trivially amplifiable.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate 11\nFile: `nextjs/middleware.ts`\nLine: 52\nInvariant: I10\nClaim: `getBetterAuthSession()` does `fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/get-session`, { headers: { cookie } })` with no `signal`. The `/api/auth/get-session` lambda is cold or its DB connection stalls; middleware blocks on the un-timed-out fetch. The whole request hangs until Vercel kills it — the admin sees a hard platform timeout page rather than the intended `catch { return null }` → redirect-to-sign-in. (Not a literal `fetch()` in scope files, but the same failure mode and the same fix surface.)\nFailure scenario: `importLicenseFromPolar` is reached from `POST /api/license/validate` — **unauthenticated**, IP-rate-limited at only 30/min — on any DB miss. An attacker (or a bad client) sending 30/min of random non-existent license keys drives 30/min of Polar API calls; if Polar stalls, each one pins a Vercel function for its full `maxDuration`. The paying user hitting `/api/license/validate` at the same moment gets a 504, and because the Fly service treats a non-2xx/timeout as \"cannot validate\" it fails the transcription closed — a real customer cannot transcribe while Polar is degraded, even though their license is in the local DB.\nConfidence: medium\n\n## Platform ceilings (blast-radius note for the verifier)\n\n- **Fly (`hyperwhisper-cloud`): no ceiling.** `fly.prod.toml` sets only `memory = '1gb'` / `cpus = 1` — no `[http_service]` request timeout, no `kill_timeout` override, no concurrency `hard_limit`/`soft_limit`. `src/index.ts` exports a plain `{ port, fetch, websocket }` Bun object with no `idleTimeout`. So candidates 1-8 are genuinely unbounded: a hung LLM fetch leaks the request scope for the machine's lifetime, and the 1 GB/1 CPU sizing means accumulation to OOM-kill is realistic. This is the material half of the finding.\n- **Vercel (`nextjs`): bounded by the platform default only.** `vercel.json` has no `functions` block and no `maxDuration`; a repo-wide grep for `maxDuration` finds hits only in generated `.next/types/**` stubs (never in source). Candidates 9-12 therefore fail *bounded* — they burn a full function invocation and surface as a 504 rather than leaking indefinitely. The invariant holds.\n- **`nextjs/app/api/download/route.ts` outbound appcast fetch — refuted.** Line 52 carries `signal: AbortSignal.timeout(APPCAST_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)` with `APPCAST_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000` and an explanatory comment about not holding a serverless function open. No timer is ever attached.
